      children: ["\n", _jsxs(_components.p, {
        children: [_jsx(_components.strong, {
          children: "置信度（confidence）"
        }), "\n模型在检测过程中，一个目标上可能会出现多个框，每一个框会对应一个 confidence，confidence 阈值就是之后计算 TP，FP，TN，FN 等参数的界限；\n", _jsx(_components.strong, {
          children: "交并比（IOU）"
        }), "\n计算模型预测出的框和标签中标注的框的 IOU，IOU 用于判定目标是真目标还是假目标，大于 IOU 阈值的框会认定为 True，小于 IOU 阈值的检测框会认定为 False；\n计算 TP，FP，TN，FN"]
      }), "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
        children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
          children: "TP：大于 IOU 阈值且大于 confidence 阈值（实际是正样本，判定为正样本）；（检测对了）"
        }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
          children: "FP：小于 IOU 阈值且大于 confidence 阈值（实际是负样本，判定为正样本）；（检测错了）"
        }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
          children: "TN：小于 IOU 阈值且小于 confidence 阈值（实际是负样本，判定为负样本）；（不用于计算）"
        }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
          children: "FN：大于 IOU 阈值且小于 confidence 阈值（实际是正样本，判定为负样本）；（没检测出来）"
        }), "\n"]
      }), "\n"]
    }), "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
      children: ["\n", _jsxs(_components.li, {
        children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.p, {
          children: "文本检测"
        }), "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
          children: ["\n", _jsxs(_components.li, {
            children: ["基于 IoU 计算（预测区域和标记区域的交并集）", "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
              children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
                children: "Precision: TP / (TP + FP) 查准率"
              }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
                children: "Recall: TP / (TP + FN) 查全率，TP + FN 为所有正样本的数量"
              }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
                children: "hmean：P 与 R 的调和平均数"
              }), "\n"]
            }), "\n"]
          }), "\n"]
        }), "\n"]
      }), "\n", _jsxs(_components.li, {
        children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.p, {
          children: "文本内容识别"
        }), "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
          children: ["\n", _jsxs(_components.li, {
            children: ["基于混淆矩阵计算（本质上是一个分类问题）", "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
              children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
                children: "Accuracy"
              }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
                children: "Precision"
              }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
                children: "Recall"
              }), "\n"]
            }), "\n"]
          }), "\n", _jsxs(_components.li, {
            children: ["基于编辑距离/莱文斯坦距离（Levenshtein）计算", "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
              children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
                children: "1-N.E.D"

function MDXContent(props = {}) {
  const {wrapper: MDXLayout} = {
    ..._provideComponents(),
    ...props.components
  };
  return MDXLayout ? _jsx(MDXLayout, {
    ...props,
    children: _jsx(_createMdxContent, {
      ...props
    })
  }) : _createMdxContent(props);
}
return {
  default: MDXContent
};
function _missingMdxReference(id, component) {
  throw new Error("Expected " + (component ? "component" : "object") + " `" + id + "` to be defined: you likely forgot to import, pass, or provide it.");
